# Master Asset Reference — Framer Project
Generated on 12 September 2026. This is an inventory of assets and systems accessible within the current Framer project. No external reference-site assets were inspected or downloaded.

## Inventory Coverage
- Pages: 11; layout templates: 2; reusable components: 27; CMS collections: 3.
- URL-backed visual/media references: 406; native icons: 6; shaders/decorative surfaces: 78; animation/effect-bearing layers: 283.
- Breakpoints found across every route and shared layout: Desktop (≥1360px), Tablet (810–1359.98px), Phone (≤809.98px). Component variants are separately enumerated below.

## Reference Asset Availability
No external reference website was supplied or scanned for this inventory. Therefore no unavailable reference asset has been added. Any future external-only asset should be recorded as: **REFERENCE ASSET — NOT AVAILABLE IN PROJECT — REPLACEMENT REQUIRED**.

## Notes for Rebuild
- Image/video URLs in the manifest are project-accessible references found in fields or layer properties; their availability outside Framer depends on the hosting URL and rights held by the project owner.
- Native Framer gradients, masks, shader layers, variants, effects, layout-template content, and breakpoint overrides are documented as rebuild specifications rather than exportable binary files.
- Font names are documented; no font file redistribution is asserted.

## Responsive System
- Desktop: min-width 1360px. Tablet: 810px through 1359.98px. Phone: max-width 809.98px.
- Every route has Desktop, Tablet, and Phone variants. Component responsive variants are enumerated in the component catalog.
- Native breakpoints, layout-template inheritance, variants, shaders, masks, gradients, and effects are available in the project but NOT ACCESSIBLE / NOT EXPORTABLE as independent binary files.

## Asset Accessibility
- Font files: NOT ACCESSIBLE / NOT EXPORTABLE. Confirmed project font family names: Sora and Inter.
- Hosted image/video URLs: individually recorded in the Master Asset Manifest; these are references, not a guarantee of download rights.
- No external reference-site assets were imported into this documentation.
